\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{ADDR\_TILEINFO}{ADDR\_TILEINFO}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily typedef struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structrocr_1_1__ADDR__TILEINFO}{rocr\+::\+\_\+\+ADDR\+\_\+\+TILEINFO}} \mbox{\hyperlink{namespacerocr_a22dcb0c31be418f31f99d0311c45522c}{rocr\+::\+ADDR\+\_\+\+TILEINFO}}}



Bank/tiling parameters. On function input, these can be set as desired or left 0 for Addr\+Lib to calculate/default. On function output, these are the actual parameters used. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
Valid bank\+Width/bank\+Height value\+: 1,2,4,8. They are factors instead of pixels or bytes.
\end{DoxyNote}
The bank number remains constant across each row of the macro tile as each pipe is selected, so the number of tiles in the x direction with the same bank number will be bank\+\_\+width $\ast$ num\+\_\+pipes. \mbox{\Hypertarget{namespacerocr_a0aead2b3578575d4645d78ac2c0c72a4}\label{namespacerocr_a0aead2b3578575d4645d78ac2c0c72a4}}

Helper function to check one format can be EXPORT\+\_\+\+NUM, which is a register CB\+\_\+\+COLOR\+\_\+\+INFO.\+SURFACE\+\_\+\+FORMAT. FP16 can be reported as EXPORT\+\_\+\+NORM for rv770 in r600 family. 

Elem\+Get\+Export\+Norm

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}
The implementation is only for r600. 00 -\/ EXPORT\+\_\+\+FULL\+: PS exports are 4 pixels with 4 components with 32-\/bits-\/per-\/component. (two clocks per export) 01 -\/ EXPORT\+\_\+\+NORM\+: PS exports are 4 pixels with 4 components with 16-\/bits-\/per-\/component. (one clock per export)
\end{DoxyNote}
